Today's sharp top border looks messy where it meets the sections region. Pairs with `chat-panel-pinned-bottom` (the chat region the shadow applies to) and `chat-panel-expand-to-editor` (agent-tab body needs the same treatment). CSS-only; no behavior change |
| `chat-new-session-reveals-panel` | done | `app/src/actions.rs::new_chat_session` calls `crate::chat::session::create_new`, then `ensure_panel_visible(PANEL_CHAT)` to insert the chat panel into the dock if it was removed, and sets `state.workbench.secondary_side_bar.visible = true` because the chat surface lives in the workbench's secondary side bar in the egui layout — re-inserting alone doesn't un-collapse a hidden side bar |


## Task queue (task-queue.md)

Unified work queue for non-interactive LLM work — note mutations, RAPTOR / fan-out, background single-shots. Producers submit; consumers are the in-process direct-LLM worker and any rmcp client (external HTTP, or the basic chat agent in-process when `expose_to_chat_agent` is on). Lands as a sibling to `core::llm` and `core::agent`. Spec is `task-queue.md`.

| Slug | Status | Evidence | Notes |
| ---- | ------ | -------- | ----- |
| `task-queue-core-module` | done | `core/src/tasks.rs` (`Queue`, `Slot`, broadcast event channel, lease table) | new `core::tasks` module (queue + lease table + event emitter) wired into `VaultSession` at the host |
| `task-queue-scope-non-chat` | partial | `core/src/tasks.rs` (`TaskKind` enum); `submit_note_mutation` producer in the host | note-mutation menu (the first non-chat producer) now flows through the queue; RAPTOR / auto-tag / summary-on-save producers are still planned, so the slug stays partial until they land |
| `task-queue-task-shape` | done | `core/src/tasks.rs` (`Task`, `TaskKind`, `TaskShape`, `Priority`, `TaskPayload`) | exhaustive `TaskKind` enum + `TaskShape::{Direct, Agent}` + `Priority::{Low, Normal, High}` |
| `task-queue-shape-routing` | done | `core/src/tasks.rs::pick_next` (skips `Agent` shape for direct lane), `Queue::checkout_mcp` (no shape filter beyond opt-in) | direct worker only takes `Direct`; MCP clients can take either shape |
| `task-queue-io-worker-lane` | planned | a dedicated in-process worker for long-running non-LLM I/O (web crawl, `crawl-task-queue-lane`); distinct from the direct-LLM drain and the synchronous `NonLlmHandlers` path; never an MCP client; no `output_schema`; on the queue for lease/progress/cancel/visibility; per-page tasks roll up via `task-queue-task-grouping` |
| `task-queue-priority-tiers` | done | `core/src/tasks.rs::Priority::rank`, `pick_next` ordering | strict High/Normal/Low ordering with FIFO inside via `submitted_at` |
| `task-queue-raptor-summarize` | partial | Enum variant + payload (Sprint A) plus a producer side (Sprint D): `cluster_regenerate_names` submits one task per non-user-edited cluster node at `Priority::Normal`. **Gap:** no concrete LLM-backed worker that consumes the task yet — the direct-LLM drain receives it but the worker that resolves members + writes the summary back through `Trees::set_summary` + `reset_churn` is the next chunk. Retry rides producer-side error handling |
| `task-queue-raptor-triage-match` | done | Enum variant + payload (Sprint A), producer side (Sprint D Phase 1) — `cluster_triage_enqueue` submits one `RaptorTriageMatch` task per saved-as-triage tree at `Priority::Normal` — and consumer side (Sprint D fix-up): the direct worker checks a `NonLlmHandlers` side-channel before falling back to the LLM (`core/src/tasks.rs::NonLlmHandlers::try_handle` + `drive_one_task`). `the host's `DirectWorkerHandlers`` implements the trait and dispatches `RaptorTriageMatch` to `core::suggest::triage_match` with the session-scoped trees / vault / staging / store handles and the live `[suggestions.triage]` config — same classifier the synchronous on-save fast path uses. Payload schema carries `tree_id` + `source_path` on the variant itself (no prompt body required). Author class is hardcoded `User` for now — see `triage-author-class` (partial) for the agent-author lift. Cancel-by-source-path remains a producer-side concern |
| `raptor-summarize-sample-merge` | partial | `core/src/cluster.rs::plan_sample_merge` + `SampleMergePlan { Single, SampleAndMerge { batches }, TooLarge { member_count } }` | pure planner: members partitioned into 30-per-batch siblings above the threshold, with a 300-member cap that routes to `TooLarge` (producer skips summarization for that cluster — no fallback path). Constants `SAMPLE_MERGE_BATCH_THRESHOLD` / `BATCH_SIZE` / `MEMBER_CAP` exported for producer reuse. **Gap:** no fan-in machinery in `core::tasks` yet — sibling batches submit independently; the merge step is the producer's responsibility (matches `task-queue-task-grouping` being deferred). When the producer lands, each batch is a `RaptorSummarize` task; the merge is a separate `RaptorSummarize` whose payload aggregates the partial summaries |
| `task-queue-lifecycle` | done | `core/src/tasks.rs::TaskState`, `tick_maintenance` (lease-expiry requeue) | `Queued → Leased → Completed/Failed/Cancelled` with expiry-requeue path |
| `task-queue-terminal-retention` | done | `core/src/tasks.rs::tick_maintenance` (GC past `terminal_retention_secs`); ticked every 2s from the host | terminal rows kept for `terminal_retention_secs` (default 60) then GC'd |
| `task-queue-lease-timeout` | done | `core/src/tasks.rs::checkout_mcp` (clamps to `[1, max_secs]`), `heartbeat` extends, `tick_maintenance` requeues | external checkout stamps a lease + heartbeat extends; expiry requeues |
| `task-queue-submit-handle` | done | `core/src/tasks.rs::TaskHandle::await_outcome` | producer gets a handle awaitable for `TaskOutcome`; drop is *not* implicit cancel (deviates from spec — see notes) |
| `task-queue-event-stream` | done | `core/src/tasks.rs::QueueEvent`; emitted onto queue events from the host (broadcast→host bridge) | full event lifecycle on a single queue events channel |
| `task-queue-cancel-app-only` | done | `core/src/tasks.rs::Queue::cancel` + `tasks_cancel` command (the host) | in-process function only; not exposed as an MCP tool |
| `task-queue-cancel-propagation-internal` | done | `core/src/tasks.rs::Queue::cancel` fires the leased `StopSignal`; the direct worker observes via `tokio::select!` in `call_with_cancel` | mirrors `agent-chat-command-surface` cancel shape |
| `task-queue-stale-lease-rejection` | done | `core/src/tasks.rs::Queue::cancel` flips state to `Cancelled` while resolving the producer handle; subsequent `submit_result`/`fail` return `QueueError::StaleLease`; MCP layer maps that to error code `1006` | producer handle resolves immediately; client learns at submit time |
| `task-queue-cancel-not-via-mcp` | done | `mcp-server/src/handler.rs` — no `task_cancel` tool advertised | cancel intentionally not in the MCP surface |
| `task-queue-direct-worker` | done | `core/src/tasks.rs::run_direct_worker`; spawned per-parallelism in the host | tokio task drains `Direct`-shape tasks via `core::llm::chat` |
| `task-queue-direct-worker-toggle` | done | `core/src/config.rs::DirectWorkerConfig::enabled`; gate at the host (only spawns when enabled + `[llm] enabled`) | `[tasks] direct_worker.enabled` (default true) |
| `task-queue-structured-output` | done | `core/src/tasks.rs::validate_against_schema` (subset JSON Schema validator); MCP `task_submit_inner` validates before completing | optional `output_schema`; lightweight schema validator (top-level `type` + `required` + nested `properties`) — full Draft-2020 deferred until a producer needs it |

## Diff (diff.md)

Unified diff primitive `DiffLayer` produces hunks + decorations from two ropes; consumed by every diff surface (dirty-buffer toggle, op-log history browser, snapshot review, inline agent patch-review, whole-file staging review). Diff is a mode of the unified `buffer` tab kind, not a separate tab kind; `DiffSource` picks the comparison target.

| Slug | Status | Notes |
| ---- | ------ | ----- |
| `diff-core-module` | done | `core/src/diff.rs` (`compute`, `DiffResult`, `DiffHunk`, `DiffLine`, `DiffOp`). Pure text → diff using `similar`'s `TextDiff::from_lines` + `grouped_ops(3)`; `similar` confined to the module |
| `diff-layer` | done | `editor/editor-diff/src/lib.rs` (`DiffLayer`, `DiffOwner`). Constructor takes base + current + owner; `decorations(line_height, theme, intraline)` runs the existing `unified_decorations_opts` and returns a `DecorationSet`. Every diff path in the app routes through this — `panels/buffer/diff_overlay.rs` (diff is a mode of the editor tab via `DiffSource` in `app/src/tab.rs`) |
| `diff-as-mode` | done | the editor tab carries `Option<DiffSource>`; `diff_overlay::compute` resolves the source and pushes a `DiffLayer`-derived layer onto the same editor widget. Diff toggle in the toolbar flips the active tab's `diff` field; no separate diff-tab kind |
| `diff-source-enum` | done | `app/src/tab.rs` (`DiffSource::{ Disk, LiveBuffer, ChangesDb, StagingProposal, Trash, Empty }`). Each variant resolves to a base rope through existing services (`vault.read_file`, `changes.content_at`, `staging.content`, `fs::read_to_string`) in `diff_overlay::resolve_source_text` |
| `diff-layer-owner` | done | `DiffOwner::{ Index, Staging, Agent, Snapshot, Manual }`. `diff_overlay::compute` picks the owner from buffer state (Agent for hydrated proposals) or the DiffSource shape. Agent owner additionally emits per-hunk Accept/Reject ActionRow widgets via `attach_agent_hunk_widgets` |
| `diff-layer-hunk-widgets` | done | `app/src/panels/buffer/diff_overlay.rs` — `attach_agent_hunk_widgets` (Accept/Reject per agent hunk) + `attach_snapshot_hunk_widgets` (Restore per snapshot hunk). Click ids map through `DiffOverlay.click_map` to `HunkAction::{Accept, Reject, Restore}`. Buffer panel dispatches: `handle_hunk_accept` / `handle_hunk_reject` walk the contributing proposals; `handle_hunk_restore` splices the snapshot's byte-range text into the on-disk content via `vault.write_file_checked` and appends a `Modified` audit row |
| `diff-viewer-input-shape` | done | `app/src/tab.rs` (`DiffSource` — the diff input). `{ before, after }` — actions + close move out of the renderer's contract since each consumer's preview pane owns its own banner + lifecycle |
| `diff-viewer-line-unified` | done | whole-file paint with red/green per-line decorations. `core::diff::compute` returns one hunk containing every line so the user sees full file context with changes inline. The between-hunks `⋯` code path is in place but unreachable until `diff-viewer-grouped-hunks` lands |
| `diff-viewer-grouped-hunks` | planned | future `core::diff` entry point emits multiple hunks with bounded context (~3 lines per side); `⋯` separators + click-to-expand land then. Triggered by large-file ergonomics or `mcp-tool-diff`. Wire format already accommodates a list of hunks |
| `editor-toolbar-mode-controls` | done | centered slot between flex spacers; populated with mode-specific label + icon-only buttons whenever the active buffer's tab carries a `DiffSource` that owns verbs (snapshot restore, staging accept/reject). Idempotent rebuild via `replaceChildren()` on every transition |
| `diff-viewer-respects-dirty-source` | done | entering diff mode is not a buffer swap — the tab's `current` is unchanged, decorations layer on top — so `file-switch-guard-dirty` doesn't fire and dirty-buffer state is preserved across mode toggles |
| `editor-diff-vs-disk-toggle` | done | toolbar diff toggle. Greyed when buffer is clean *and* `DiffSource` is `Disk(path)` *and* the path exists. Click toggles the tab's diff mode against the current `DiffSource` (default `Disk(path)`). Right-click opens a source picker: `Diff against on-disk`, `Show changes…` (submenu), future sources |
| `editor-show-changes-menu` | done | `app/src/panels/buffer/show_changes.rs` (`show_diff_source_menu`). Right-click on the diff toolbar button opens "Diff against on-disk" + "Show changes…" submenu of up to 20 recent accepted ops for the path (timestamp / op-kind / author, via `op_writes::path_history`). Selecting opens (or focuses) a snapshot tab keyed on the op's `op_id`; "Browse all…" routes to the activity-row detail page. Restore-this-hunk verb on Snapshot hunks pending (`diff-layer-hunk-widgets`) |
| `cli-diff` | planned | `hiker diff <path> [<change-id>]` over `core::diff::compute`; lands when the CLI is fleshed out |
| `mcp-tool-diff` | planned | reserved as deferred — agents can already retrieve two blobs (`get_note` + `change_content`) and reason over them |
| `activity-detail-diff-between-versions` | planned | multi-select two activity rows + "Diff selected"; opens a `buffer` tab with `current = content_at(id_a)`, `diff = Some(ChangesDb(id_b))`; depends on activity-detail multi-select |
| `diff-viewer-split-view` | planned | side-by-side layout option on `DiffLayer` (two editor widgets sharing the same hunks). Same primitive; different layout |
| `diff-viewer-intraline` | done | character-level highlights inside paired delete/insert lines. Renderer adds intra mark decorations on top of the line backgrounds |
| `diff-intraline-core-pair` | done | `core::diff::compute_intraline(before_line, after_line) -> Vec<IntralineSpan>` plus `compute_with_intraline` that runs the per-pair walk over the line list. core/src/diff.rs:111, core/src/diff.rs:71 |
| `diff-intraline-char-level-v1` | done | v1 ships char-level via `similar::TextDiff::diff_chars`; `IntralineSpan` reserves both before/after byte-offset ranges so word-level can drop in. core/src/diff.rs:42 |
| `diff-intraline-ipc-flag` | done | `compute_diff` takes optional `intraline` arg (default false); `DiffLine.intraline_spans` skips serialization when None. core/src/diff.rs:58 |
| `diff-intraline-render-marks` | done | renderer emits `.cm-diff-add-intra` / `.cm-diff-del-intra` mark decorations keyed by line + UTF-8 byte offsets on top of the line decorations |
| `view-intraline-diff-toggle` | done | "Intraline diff highlights" View-menu entry flips `editor.intraline_diff`; affects every `DiffLayer` consumer. Toggling while a diff is rendered re-runs the compute |
| `diff-viewer-three-way` | planned | third rope slot on `DiffLayer` for merge / drift-conflict resolution; anchors `drift-conflict-modal`'s deferred "open diff" option |
| `diff-viewer-ignore-whitespace` | planned | toggle on the `compute` call to normalize whitespace before diffing |
| `diff-viewer-export-patch` | planned | "copy as unified-diff patch" affordance |


## Op log (op-log.md)

The substrate every write rides on: one CRDT-shaped op log per document, with per-op author and status. The Yrs two-doc model, pending queue, side table, atomic-write/materialization path, history materialization, compaction, and the `[op-log]` config section live in `core::oplog`; producers wire through `core::ops::op_writes`.

| Slug | Status | Notes |
| ---- | ------ | ----- |
| `op-log-yrs-backed` | done | `yrs = "0.26"` added to `Cargo.toml` + `core/Cargo.toml`, confined to `core::oplog`. One Yrs `Doc` per document (`core/src/oplog/doc.rs::new_doc`); whole `.md` is a single `text: Y.Text` (byte-offset positions), no structural frontmatter modeling; per-doc `client_id` recorded on every side-table row |
| `op-log-document-identity` | planned | one Yrs Doc per document; native md uses the .md path, non-md sources use the sidecar, externals use `.hiker/external/<id>--slug.md` |
| `op-log-sidecar-document` | planned | sidecars are the CRDT unit for non-md sources; the source itself is read-only as far as the log is concerned |
| `op-log-external-handle` | planned | `myrepo://docs/foo.md`-style logical handles for source pointers outside the vault; resolved per-device via user-scope `[external_roots]` config (not synced) |
| `op-log-document-shape` | done | `core/src/oplog/doc.rs:42` (`new_doc`/`seed_doc`/`materialize`) — Yrs Doc layout `text: Y.Text` + `meta: Y.Map {kind, path, tombstone}`, byte-offset text positions; frontmatter plain text inside `text`, no YAML re-serialization. Tested at `core/src/oplog/tests.rs` (`materialize_round_trips_verbatim_with_frontmatter`, `frontmatter_keys_comments_scalars_survive_byte_for_byte`) |
| `op-log-layered-model` | done | Buffer = merge of three CRDT op layers `accepted + working + pending`; `materialize_working` (accepted+working) is the editable buffer, `materialize_review` (accepted+working+pending) the overlay's current side. `core/src/oplog/{mod.rs,working.rs}`; app binding `app/src/panels/buffer/editor_binding.rs`. Tested in `oplog/tests.rs`. |
| `op-log-working-layer` | done | User's uncommitted edits as `user` ops on top of `accepted`; `DocState.working: Option<Doc>`. `working.rs` accessors (`apply_working_edit`/`materialize_working`/`materialize_review`/`discard_working`/`has_working_edits`) + `mod.rs::commit_working` (Save → fold into `accepted`). Tested: `working_edit_shows_in_buffer_not_on_disk`, `commit_working_folds_into_accepted`, `accept_preserves_working_edits`, `external_edit_preserves_working_edits`. |
| `op-log-editor-binding` | done | Op-level editor↔CRDT sync. Editor seam: `editor-view` `Action::Replace { state, tx }` + `editor-egui::Widget::with_transactions_sink`. App `editor_binding.rs`: forward `change_set_edits` (walk `Set::ops()`) → `apply_working_edit`; reverse `materialize_working` → `set_doc_clamping_selection`; overlay → `materialize_review`. Host-applied edits bypass the sink → no echo. Tested `editor-egui/tests/transactions_out.rs` + `editor_binding` unit tests. |
| `op-log-merge-auto` | done | Disjoint `working` and `pending` edits merge by position automatically (both render, no prompt) — falls out of the layered model. Tested `review_view_overlays_pending_on_working`, `accept_preserves_working_edits`. |
| `op-log-merge-conflict` | done | Overlapping `working` + `pending` edits surface as a conflict hunk (`ActionTone::Conflicted`) with Keep mine / Keep theirs / Keep both, rather than a silent positional interleave. Detection + keep-theirs revert args are pure fns in `app/src/panels/buffer/conflict.rs` (tested); the three verbs wire through `app/src/panels/buffer/diff_overlay.rs` (`conflict_row`) → `patch_review.rs` (`apply_hunk_keep_theirs`). Keep-mine rejects the pending op; keep-both accepts it (CRDT interleaves); keep-theirs reverts the user's overlapping `working` edit to accepted text via `apply_working_edit`, then accepts. |
| `op-log-pending-queue` | done | `core/src/oplog/shapes.rs::PendingOp` + `mod.rs::stage_pending` (producer step: materialize → resolve old_str byte range → translate on clone → serialize update; stashes `old_str`/`new_str` in op metadata). Drift in `mod.rs::is_pending_drifted` — anchored ops drift when their `old_str` no longer resolves in current `accepted` (CRDT can still merge the bytes, so a contains-check is too weak); anchorless ops fall back to `doc.rs::applies_cleanly`. `doc.rs::produce_replace`/`produce_body_rewrite`. Tested `tests.rs` (`drift_detected_when_accepted_advances`, `anchor_conflict_on_missing_old_str`, `whole_body_rewrite_keeps_frontmatter`) |
| `op-log-side-table` | done | `core/src/oplog/meta.rs::open_meta` — `op_metadata` table + three indexes exactly as specced, WAL + `synchronous=NORMAL`, fail-loud on version mismatch (`SCHEMA_VERSION=1`). `query_metadata` returns `OpMetadata` DTOs. Tested `tests.rs` (`query_filters_by_author_class_and_doc`, `version_mismatch_fails_loud`) |
| `op-log-author-classes` | done | `core/src/oplog/shapes.rs::Author` — full vocab (user / agent / external / extractor / auto / sync) with `as_wire`/`parse`/`class`; `meta.rs::Filter.author_class` builds a `(author = 'class' OR author LIKE 'class:%')` prefix match — catches both bare authors (`user`, `external`) and `class:id` authors (`agent:claude-code`). Tested `tests.rs` (`author_wire_round_trips`, `query_filters_by_author_class_and_doc`) |
| `op-log-status-states` | done | `core/src/oplog/meta.rs::OpStatus` (accepted/rejected) + `mod.rs::{accept_pending, reject_pending}` + `gc_metadata`; pending lives only in `<doc-id>.pending`. `auto_reject_on_drift` now wired: `mod.rs::auto_reject_drifted` rejects every drifted pending op (via `is_pending_drifted`); seam `ops/op_writes.rs::auto_reject_drifted` gates on the config flag; fired post-save at `app/src/editor_pane.rs::save_buffer`. Tested (`accept_applies_to_accepted_and_disk`, `reject_discards_and_writes_audit_row`, `gc_removes_old_rejected_rows`, `ops/tests.rs::auto_reject_on_drift_flips_a_drifted_op`) |
| `op-log-agent-replica` | done | `core/src/oplog/doc.rs::produce_replace`/`produce_body_rewrite` build the update against a clone of `accepted`; the clone is discarded, bytes queue as pending; `accept_pending` applies. Tested `tests.rs::accept_applies_to_accepted_and_disk` |
| `op-log-pending-survives-restart` | done | `core/src/oplog/store.rs::{save_pending, load_pending}` (bincode `Vec<PendingOp>` in `<doc-id>.pending`); `ensure_loaded` reconstitutes on reopen. Tested `tests.rs::pending_survives_restart` (serialize to temp dir, reopen, accept) |
| `op-log-hunk-view` | done | core seam `core/src/ops/op_writes.rs::review_materializations` returns `(materialize_accepted, materialize_pending_view(session))`. The app now hydrates the buffer off it: `app/src/buffer.rs::hydrate_pending_view` sets `editor.doc = pending_view`, `agent_base = accepted`; `app/src/editor_pane.rs::open_file` calls it on open; the inline `DiffLayer { owner: Agent }` diffs `agent_base` vs `editor.doc` (`app/src/panels/buffer/diff_overlay.rs`). No `staging.db` read on this path. Tested `ops/tests.rs::hunk_accept_applies_only_overlapping_ops` |
| `op-log-per-hunk-accept-reject` | done | `op_writes.rs::ops_in_hunk` resolves a hunk's byte range → pending op ids; `flip_op_status` → `accept_pending`/`reject_pending`. The inline UI now rides these: `diff_overlay.rs::attach_agent_hunk_widgets` calls `ops_in_hunk` per hunk (drift-aware Accept-disable via `is_pending_drifted`); `app/src/panels/buffer/patch_review.rs::{apply_hunk_accept, apply_hunk_reject, apply_pill_action}` flip through `op_writes::flip_op_status`. Accept-all skips drifted ops, Reject-all covers them. No `staging.accept/reject` on this path. Tested `ops/tests.rs::{hunk_accept_applies_only_overlapping_ops, hunk_reject_leaves_accepted_untouched}` |
| `op-log-per-op-status-flip` | done | `core/src/ops/op_writes.rs::flip_op_status` → `OpLog::{accept_pending, reject_pending}` is the per-op primitive both per-hunk verbs ride on (sub-hunk UI deferred). Tested `ops/tests.rs::{stage_agent_edit_then_flip_op_status, hunk_accept_applies_only_overlapping_ops, hunk_reject_leaves_accepted_untouched}` |
| `op-log-disk-canonical` | done | `core/src/oplog/mod.rs::write_md` writes `materialize(accepted).text` bytes verbatim; materialization is the identity over `text` so disk == accepted by construction. Tested `tests.rs` (`materialize_round_trips_verbatim_with_frontmatter` asserts on-disk file bytes, `accept_applies_to_accepted_and_disk`) |
| `op-log-store-layout` | done | `core/src/oplog/store.rs` (`yrs_path`/`pending_path`/`save_*`/`load_*`) + `meta.rs::{open_meta, open_index}` — `<vault>/.hiker/oplog/<doc-id>.yrs` + `<doc-id>.pending` (bincode `Vec<PendingOp>`) + `oplog_meta.db` + `doc-index.db`; write-temp-then-rename + fsync. Tested `tests.rs::doc_index_maps_path_to_id` |
| `op-log-compaction` | done | `core/src/oplog/store.rs::needs_compaction` + `mod.rs::compact_all_on_open` — rewrites `<doc-id>.yrs` as a fresh full-state snapshot when its size > `compact_threshold ×` materialized size, checked on open. Tested `tests.rs::compaction_rewrites_oversized_snapshot_on_open` |
| `op-log-materialization` | done | `core/src/oplog/doc.rs::materialize` — pure read returning `{ text, tombstone }`, `text` verbatim (no parse/re-emit); surfaced as `OpLog::materialize_accepted`/`materialize_pending_view` → `DocContent`. Tested `tests.rs::materialize_round_trips_verbatim_with_frontmatter` |

Wired at `app/src/bootstrap.rs::Spawner::oplog_external_sync_relay` (subscribes to the watcher, `.md` Created/Modified only). ## Patch review (patch-review.md)

Inline review of pending agent ops on the active buffer. Buffer renders `materialize(pending_view(session))`; the diff against `materialize(accepted)` (= `agent_base`) renders as hunks via `DiffLayer`. Per-hunk accept applies the contributing pending ops' Yrs updates to `accepted`; reject drops them from the queue. Substrate in `op-log.md`. Current implementation rides legacy `core::staging` + buffer-hydration; rows below describe the intended op-log shape with current state noted.

| Slug | Status | Notes |
| ---- | ------ | ----- |
| `patch-review-buffer-state` | done | `app/src/buffer.rs::hydrate_pending_view` sets `editor.doc = materialize(pending_view(active_session))` and `agent_base = materialize(accepted)` off `op_writes::review_materializations`; clears `agent_base` to `None` when the two are equal (no pending ops in scope). Called from `editor_pane::open_file` and after every flip. No `staging.db` read. The `Buffer` struct dropped `hydrated_proposals`/`hydration_footprints`/`post_hydration_hash`, gained `active_session` |
| `patch-review-buffer-is-view` | done | the buffer rope is a pure re-materialization off `<doc-id>.yrs` + `<doc-id>.pending`: open hydrates via `hydrate_pending_view`, close discards the rope (existing `close_tab` buffer drop), reopen re-materializes fresh. No hydrate-on-open / dehydrate-on-close staging dance remains |
| `patch-review-diff-layer` | done | `app/src/panels/buffer/diff_overlay.rs::compute` builds `DiffLayer { base: agent_base, current, owner: Agent }` and pushes decorations onto the editor's decoration stack. Same primitive powers diff toggle and snapshot/history viewer |
| `patch-review-per-hunk-accept` | done | `diff_overlay.rs::attach_agent_hunk_widgets` resolves each hunk's `current_range` → pending op ids via `op_writes::ops_in_hunk(rel, session, start, end)`; the Accept/Reject click routes through `app/src/panels/buffer/patch_review.rs::{apply_hunk_accept, apply_hunk_reject}` → `op_writes::flip_op_status`. After the flip the buffer re-reads disk + re-materializes the pending-view (`reload_and_rehydrate`) so resolved hunks disappear. No `staging.accept/reject` |
| `patch-review-coexisting-edits` | done | user typing produces accepted ops; the diff `base` vs `current` excludes/includes them symmetrically so user typing produces no self-diff hunks. Today this property holds via "user edits update `current` but not `hydration_footprints`"; under op-log it holds via the materialization-filter math |
| `patch-review-batch-grouping` | planned | ops sharing `metadata.batch_id` (e.g. multiple `Replace` ops from one `edit_note` call) get a thin gutter connector between sibling hunks. No coupled behavior; per-hunk accept/reject independent |
| `patch-review-multi-session` | done | `patch_review.rs::pill_meta` tallies pending ops per `session_id` off `OpLog::pending_ops`; when >1 session has pending ops the pill (`patch_review_pill.rs`) renders one selectable `Session <id>: N hunks` row each. Clicking sets `Buffer.active_session` and re-materializes the pending-view scoped to that session (`review_materializations` / `ops_in_hunk` take the session) |
| `patch-review-file-pill` | done | `app/src/panels/buffer/patch_review_pill.rs` consumes `Vec<HunkInfo>` from the diff overlay; renders `N agent hunks (M drifted)` + Accept-all / Reject-all / Next-hunk + per-session rows. Bulk verbs flip pending ops through `op_writes::flip_op_status` (Accept-all skips drifted, Reject-all covers them) then `reload_and_rehydrate`. Drifted count from `is_pending_drifted` |
| `patch-review-conflicted-hunk-display` | planned | conflicted proposals (hydration-time apply failures, or disk-side state from `staging-drift-eager-recheck` before hydration) surface via the file-pill's `(M conflicted)` suffix. They don't render as hunks in the inline view |
| `patch-review-conflicted-accept-disabled` | partial | inline hunk overlay: `diff_overlay.rs::attach_agent_hunk_widgets` greys the per-hunk Accept button (`enabled: false`) and labels the row `Hunk N (drifted)` when any contributing op is drifted (`OpLog::is_pending_drifted`); Reject stays active. The file pill surfaces the `(M drifted)` count. Gap: the file-pill popover + `Changes`-tab drift-reason tooltip are still planned |
| `patch-review-conflicted-list-popover` | planned | clicking the `(M conflicted)` suffix on the file pill opens a popover listing each conflicted proposal with `[Reject]` and `View` (opens the proposal in diff mode against `Empty`) |
| `patch-review-decorations` | planned | umbrella slug for the inline rendering of pending edits. Implementation now rides `patch-review-diff-layer` rather than a custom decoration provider |
| `note-open-routes-to-pending-review` | planned | when `openFile` resolves a path with at least one pending whole-file proposal, the open lands in the whole-file review surface (per `write-note-review-surface`); pending `edit_note` proposals trigger hydration into plain editing |
| `write-note-review-surface` | done | now op-log-backed (Phase 3c). Whole-file proposals are pending whole-body `Replace{anchor:None}` / `Create` ops listed via `core::ops::op_writes::list_whole_file_proposals` (per-frame cache `ui_cache.whole_file_proposals`, populated in `main::refresh_whole_file_proposals`). Open as `TabKind::Editor { buffer: StagingProposal{ proposal_id=op_id, target_path }, diff: Some(Disk{target_path}) }`. `editor_pane::ensure_readonly_buffer_loaded` + `diff_overlay::resolve_source_text` load the proposed content via `op_writes::proposal_materializations` (`materialize(accepted + just this op)` vs `materialize_accepted`) — no `staging.db` read. `panels::buffer::accept_staging_proposal` / `reject_staging_proposal` route through `op_writes::flip_op_status` → `accept_pending`/`reject_pending` on the op id |
| `write-note-review-mode-label` | planned | label reads `Review rewrite` (target path exists on disk) vs. `Review new note` (target path doesn't), with muted origin suffix (`· chat` / `· batch` / `· trail`) |
| `write-note-review-blocks-on-dirty` | planned | Accept refuses with modal when the live buffer for the target path is dirty. Reject works regardless |
| `write-note-pending-banner` | planned | thin amber strip above the editor when a buffer tab is in plain editing and its path has a pending whole-file proposal. `Review` button switches the tab into the whole-file review surface |
| `write-note-review-conflicted-display` | done | drift is read off the op log (`OpLog::is_pending_drifted`, surfaced on `WholeFileProposal.drifted`). Accept is disabled with a drift reason in the tooltip on both the whole-file review toolbar (`render_readonly_source_toolbar`) and the pending-rewrite banner (`pending_rewrite_banner`); proposed content still renders; Reject + View stay active |
| `editor-action-row-primitive` | done | `BlockKind::ActionRow { label, glyph, tone, buttons }` in `editor-core::decoration` with `ActionButton`, `ActionButtonStyle` (Primary/Danger/Neutral), `ActionTone` (Normal/Warning/Conflicted). Rendered by `paint_action_row` in `editor-egui`; each enabled button registers a per-rect `ClickZone` with `ClickAction::WidgetClick(button.id)`. Pure-data primitive used by `diff-layer-hunk-widgets` |
| `editor-inline-widget-display` | done | `InlineWidget` trait carries `fn display() -> Option<InlineWidgetDisplay>`. When `Some`, the egui painter renders the supplied `{ text, bg, fg, strikethrough }` as the segment galley + bg fill instead of the bordered placeholder |
| `editor-end-of-line-widget` | done | `build_line_layout` collects `InlineWidget` decorations whose `range.start == line_byte_end` into a trailing-segment list rather than clipping them to zero width |
| `patch-review-cross-file-deferred` | planned | multi-file `edit_note` workflows; depends on a future `multi_edit_note` tool surface |
| `patch-review-three-way-deferred` | planned | true three-way merge for whole-file accept-while-dirty; rides `diff-viewer-three-way` |
| `patch-review-per-agent-attribution-deferred` | planned | per-edit badges showing which agent issued which hunk; useful only with concurrent multi-agent proposals |
| `patch-review-hunk-keybind-deferred` | planned | global keybind for next/previous hunk in the active buffer; the file pill's Next button covers the immediate need |


## Changes log (op-log.md)

User-facing surfaces over the op log: history queries, rollback verbs, the merged activity feed. Substrate + projection spec live in `op-log.md` ("History materialization"). The op log is the sole changelog substrate; these surfaces are projections over `OpLog::query_metadata` plus content materialization via `op_writes::content_at_op`.

| Slug | Status | Notes |
| ---- | ------ | ----- |
| `changes-query-api` | done | `core/src/activity.rs::AcceptedFeed` — thin projection over `OpLog::query_metadata` returning the `ChangeRow`/`ChangeOp` DTOs (defined in the same module): `recent`/`recent_by_author`/`history_for_path` over accepted ops, `op_kind_to_change_op` maps `create→Created`, `replace`/`set_frontmatter→Modified`, `tombstone→Deleted`, `rename→Renamed`, `rename_from` carried; ulid `op_id`+`doc_id` preserved in the row's `metadata` (the `id: i64` field holds `timestamp_ms`), `is_current` stamped over the window. Path resolution via `OpLog::path_for_doc` (reads `meta.path`). Per-path version lists / content read through `core/src/ops/op_writes.rs::{path_history, content_at_op}` (`OpMetadata`-shaped). Tested `core/src/activity.rs` |
| `changes-rollback-helper` | done | `app/src/panels/home.rs::rollback_change` pulls the prior content via `op_writes::previous_accepted_content` and writes it back through `op_writes::user_save` — a fresh `user` op that becomes the newest accepted version (the original op stays in the log). Snapshot restore (`app/src/panels/buffer/mod.rs::restore_snapshot_to_disk` / `apply_hunk_restore`) reads `content_at_op` and re-saves through `user_save` the same way |
| `activity-feed-merged` | done | `core/src/activity.rs`, `core/src/lib.rs` (mod), the host (`VaultSession.activity` + `activity_list*` commands), `app/src/panels/changes.rs` (activity detail uses `activity_list`) | backend-merged feed projects over `core::oplog` — accepted ops → `Payload::Change(ChangeRow)`, pending ops → `Payload::Pending(PendingItem)`; one stream, no frontend reconciliation |
| `activity-feed-module` | done | `core/src/activity.rs` (`Activity::new(Arc<OpLog>)`), the host (`activity: Arc<Activity>` on `VaultSession`, constructed from `oplog.clone()` in `bootstrap.rs`) | pure projection — no fs/db writes; depends only on `Arc<OpLog>`. Accepted via `AcceptedFeed`, pending via `OpLog::all_pending_ops`. The `ChangeRow`/`ChangeOp`/`AcceptedFeed` DTOs live in this module. Tested `core/src/activity.rs::tests` |
| `activity-feed-unified-item` | done | `core/src/activity.rs` (`ActivityItem`, `ActivityPayload`, `ActivitySummary`) | tagged enum `kind: "change" \| "staging"` with newtype-variant flattening; the app consumes the flattened DTO directly |
| `activity-feed-source-filter` | done | `core/src/activity.rs` (`ActivitySource`, `list_inner` short-circuits when only one source is wanted) | `ChangesOnly` skips the staging query; `StagingOnly` skips changes; `Merged` runs both. `recent_changes` / `staging_list` commands kept for single-source consumers |
| `activity-feed-staging-metadata` | done | `core/src/activity.rs` (`Activity::pending_items` projects each `OpLog::all_pending_ops` op into a `PendingItem` carrying `surface`/`action`/`target_path`/`trail_id`/`session_id`/`content_hash`/`created_at_ms` + raw `metadata`, wrapped as `Payload::Pending`) | `session_id` lifted out of the op's metadata blob alongside the raw blob (consumers that already parse metadata don't break); `content_hash` derived from the op's `new_str` rather than a separate content fetch |
| `activity-feed-merged-query` | done | `core/src/activity.rs` (`Activity::list`, `list_for_path`, `count`), the host (`activity_list`, `activity_list_for_path`, `activity_count` commands) | three commands wrap the same `Activity` API; `ActivityFilter` carries `source` + `limit` + `author_pattern` + `since_ms` |
| `activity-feed-merge-ordering` | done | `core/src/activity.rs` (`list_inner` sort: `timestamp_ms desc, sort_secondary desc`; secondary key is `c:<padded id>` for changes / `s:<ulid>` for staging) | deterministic across calls; staging ULIDs sort by timestamp naturally, change ids by monotonic count. Test `merged_orders_by_timestamp_desc` covers the happy path |
| `activity-feed-activity-detail-consumer` | done | `app/src/panels/changes.rs` (activity detail) | calls `activity_list({source:"merged", limit:200})` and splits items back into the change-rows / proposals the renderer consumes. Single fetch replaces the prior pair. The author-class filter pills stay client-side (multi-select UX); a dedicated "Pending → StagingOnly" toggle pill is not added since no such pill existed pre-migration |


## Sync (sync.md)

Multi-device sync transport over the op-log substrate: identity/enrollment, the encrypted transport, and the server. Implemented as the `hiker-sync` crate (+ `core::oplog` receive verbs); **library-complete and exercised by an in-process end-to-end test suite over real libp2p TCP/Noise/yamux** (`hiker-sync/tests/{lan_sync,server_sync,scenarios}.rs`, 12 integration tests). The remaining `partial`/`planned` work is the egui app surface (enrollment page, discovery button, fork-resolution UI), the in-band vault-key handshake, and user-scope secret storage — none of which the tests need.

| Slug | Status | Evidence | Notes |
| ---- | ------ | -------- | ----- |
| `sync-negotiated-doc-ids` | done | `hiker-sync/src/{identity,transport}.rs` | local ULID `doc_id` stays local; transport binds local ids to a shared logical lineage. Scenario 1 |
| `sync-path-matching-key` | done | `hiker-sync/src/transport.rs` (`sync_entry` binding resolution) | path matches at first contact only; bound entries resolve by logical id so renames don't re-open identity. Scenario 5 |
| `sync-lineage-adoption` | done | `core/src/oplog/sync.rs::adopt_lineage` | adopt canonical base + reconcile local divergence as a `user` op (3-way merge over the shared seed). Scenario 1 |
| `sync-enrollment-hash-classification` | done | `hiker-sync/src/enroll.rs::classify` | identical / fast-forward / fork from the `.ops` content-hash history; both-directions match → fork. Scenarios 3, 4 |
| `sync-blocked-state` | done | `hiker-sync/src/{identity,transport}.rs` (`BlockedDoc` + `Resolution`, fork branch + `set_fork_resolution`, `PushAdopt` handler + `push_adopt_to_peer`), `core/src/oplog/sync.rs::adopt_lineage_theirs`, `app/src/panels/sync.rs` (Conflicts section) | fork → Blocked + no silent merge (scenario 4); persistent blocked list surfaced on the Sync page with keep-mine / keep-theirs / keep-both. All three resolve both sides in one click: keep-theirs/keep-both adopt the peer's lineage (keep-both first writes a conflict-copy note); keep-mine pushes our base so the peer adopts it (the peer's divergence discarded), establishing a shared lineage. Both-keep-mine: first push wins and clears the other's pending decision (no flap). Tests `fork_keep_theirs_converges`, `fork_keep_both_preserves_local_copy`, `fork_keep_mine_resolves_both_sides`, `fork_both_keep_mine_converges_deterministically`, `fork_resolution_no_corruption_on_extra_rounds` |
| `sync-fork-diff` | done | `hiker-sync/src/protocol.rs` (`DocContentRequest`/`DocContentResponse`); `hiker-sync/src/transport.rs` (`DocContentRequest` handler + `fetch_doc_text`); `app/src/sync_service.rs::fetch_fork_diff` (+ `ForkDiffResult` channel); `app/src/bootstrap.rs`/`main.rs::drain_fork_diff_results` (channel wiring); `app/src/state.rs` (`fork_diff_rx` + `panels.sync`); `app/src/panels/sync.rs` (`State`/`ForkDiffState`, View-diff button, `render_unified_diff` via `core::diff::compute`); test `fetch_doc_text_returns_peer_text_without_mutating_ours` | per forked-doc "View diff": fetches the peer's CURRENT accepted text on demand (forks hold ours, not theirs) over the Noise channel and renders a read-only ours-vs-theirs unified diff (capped lines, Hide toggle). Peer must be online (resolved from the discovered set by fingerprint; clear "not reachable" error otherwise). Read-only: never binds, adopts, or mutates the local doc; render path reads the UI cache, never the node |
| `sync-content-hash-column` | done | `core/src/oplog/meta.rs` (`content_hash` col, SCHEMA_VERSION 2) + `mod.rs::doc_history_hashes` | blake3 of materialized content per accepted op; indexed history-hash query |
| `sync-key-swap-enrollment` | done | `hiker-sync/src/{crypto,transport}.rs` (`fingerprint`/`enroll_peer`/`unenroll_peer` + PeerId auth gate); `app/src/panels/sync.rs` (enroll/rename/remove rows); `sync_service.rs` (aliases sidecar) | fingerprint swap + auth (scenario 7); Sync page shows this device's fingerprint (copy) and enrolled devices with local aliases + remove |
| `sync-vault-key-inband` | done | `hiker-sync/src/crypto.rs` (`ContentKey::fingerprint`, `SharedContentKey`); `hiker-sync/src/protocol.rs` (`content_key_fp` in `Hello`/`HelloAck`, `ContentKeyRequest`/`ContentKeyResponse`); `hiker-sync/src/transport.rs` (`converge_content_key` in `sync_once` + `ContentKeyRequest` handler); `app/src/sync_service.rs` (persist-through `SharedContentKey` hook + manual `import_content_key`); test `content_key_auto_transfers_on_first_contact` | automatic in-band transfer: handshake compares non-secret content-key fingerprints; on mismatch the non-canonical side (`min(device fp)`) adopts the canonical device's key over the authenticated Noise channel before any deltas, both then decrypt. Key rides a shared persist-through handle so auto + manual import both update the live node AND write through the user-scope KeyStore. Manual Copy/Import kept as a fallback |
| `sync-secrets-user-scope` | done | `app/src/sync_service.rs::KeyStore` (`<data_dir>/hiker/sync/<vault-id>/{device.key,content.key}`, 0600); `core/src/config/sections.rs` (doc note); `hiker-syncd` persists its own key | client device keypair + vault content key stored user-scope (platform data dir), never in the synced vault; `[sync]` config carries no secrets |
| `sync-vault-stable-id` | done | `core/src/vault.rs::vault_id` (`.hiker/vault-id` ULID) + `app/src/sync_service.rs::KeyStore::dir_for_vault` | key store keyed by an in-vault stable id, not the absolute path, so moving/renaming the vault keeps its sync identity + keys; best-effort migration from the legacy path-hash dir. Tests `vault_key_dir_survives_move`, `distinct_vaults_get_distinct_key_dirs` |
| `sync-libp2p-transport` | done | `hiker-sync/src/transport.rs` (`build_swarm`) | libp2p 0.56 `default-features=false` + `tcp`/`noise`/`yamux`/`mdns`/`request-response`(cbor)/`tokio`/`macros`; role-agnostic |
| `sync-banned-p2p-features` | done | `deny.toml`; verified `cargo tree` has no kad/dcutr/relay/autonat | bans enforce it in CI (cargo-deny not installed locally) |
| `sync-stream-muxing` | done | `hiker-sync/src/transport.rs` | yamux mux; multi-doc convergence in one session. Scenario 8 |
| `sync-tcp-transport-choice` | done | `hiker-sync/src/transport.rs` (`with_tcp`) | TCP for firewall reach; QUIC deferred |
| `sync-p2p-lan` | done | `hiker-sync/src/transport.rs` (`sync_once`/`dial`/`listen`) | direct peer-to-peer; authenticate from swapped fingerprints. Scenarios 1–5, 8, 9 |
| `sync-decoupled-server` | done | `hiker-sync/src/server.rs::SyncServer` + `src/bin/hiker-syncd.rs` | hub binary, standalone; in-process-alongside-app wiring is app-level (not yet) |
| `sync-zero-knowledge-server` | done | `hiker-sync/src/server.rs` (`FileBlobStore`, store-and-forward) | holds no key, can't decrypt; append-only encrypted-blob log + per-device cursor. Scenario 6 asserts ciphertext-only |
| `sync-blind-id` | done | `hiker-sync/src/{crypto.rs::blind_id, server.rs}` | blobs keyed by `HMAC(content_key, logical_id)`; residual count/size/timing leakage → `sync-metadata-padding-deferred` |
| `sync-noise-channel` | done | `hiker-sync/src/transport.rs` (`noise::Config`) | Noise XX mutual auth from enrolled static keys (no PKI), forward secrecy, replay protection. **Cipher is ChaCha20-Poly1305** — libp2p-noise 0.56 doesn't expose AES-GCM for the channel; AES-256 lives in the content layer |
| `sync-content-encryption-aes256` | done | `hiker-sync/src/crypto.rs` (`ContentKey`, Aes256Gcm) | each `update_v2` AES-256-GCM-encrypted client-side before it leaves the device; makes the server zero-knowledge. Scenario 6 |
| `sync-mdns-discovery` | done | `hiker-sync/src/transport.rs` (libp2p-mdns behaviour, continuous discovered-peer tracking + `start_discovery`) | continuous enrolled-peer discovery while `enabled && discovery` (feeds `sync-auto-sync`); manual "Discover (30s)" button remains. Tracking unit-tested; live-multicast e2e not run (sandbox) |
| `sync-auto-sync` | done | `app/src/bootstrap.rs` (auto-driver in the responder `select!`) + `sync_service.rs::{run_sync_round, auto_sync_round}` | always-converge while enabled: startup + ~15s interval + on-peer-discovered rounds; only deltas transferred; empty rounds silent; gated by `discovery` + the kill switch. Two-node auto-convergence test (no manual `sync_once`) in `sync_service.rs` |
| `sync-discovered-peers` | done | `hiker-sync/src/transport.rs` (single discovered map, read-time classification) + `app/src/panels/sync.rs` | discovered peers classified enrolled-target vs seen-unenrolled at READ time against the live shared enrolled set, so enrolling promotes to a sync target immediately (no mDNS re-announce wait); page shows "Discovered on LAN (enrolled)" + "Seen on LAN (not enrolled)" + a one-time log line per newly-seen un-enrolled peer. Tests `enrolling_a_seen_peer_promotes_it_to_candidate`, `enroll_promotes_already_seen_peer_for_next_round` |
| `sync-enroll-from-discovered` | done | `hiker-sync/src/crypto.rs::peer_id_to_fingerprint` + `app/src/panels/sync.rs` | each "Seen on LAN" row shows the fingerprint derived from the peer's Ed25519 identity-multihash `PeerId` + an Enroll button (verify-the-fingerprint caption; not blind trust); enrolling kicks an immediate round. Test `peer_id_to_fingerprint_round_trips` |
| `sync-config-section` | done | `core/src/config/sections.rs::SyncSection` + `mod.rs` registration | per-vault `[sync]` block: `enabled`/`mode`/`server_url`/`discovery`/`devices`; secrets excluded |
| `sync-ui-page` | done | `app/src/panels/sync.rs` + `tab.rs`/`toolbar.rs`/`tabs.rs` (`TabKind::Sync`) | Sync tab: state grid, copyable fingerprint, content-key copy/import, enrolled devices (alias + rename + remove), discovered-peer buckets + one-click Enroll (`sync-discovered-peers`/`sync-enroll-from-discovered`), Conflicts section (keep-mine/theirs/both), config-sanity warnings, manual peer-address connect, last-error line, in-page **Enable/Disable sync** buttons, Sync-now/Discover, recently-synced items + progress log; "Enable sync" button (not a dead-end hint) when off |
| `sync-settings-section` | done | `app/src/panels/settings/mod.rs::sync_section` + `core/src/config/patch.rs` (`sync.*` eligible, vault scope) | `enabled`/`mode`/`server_url`/`discovery` rows; `devices` read-only (enroll on the page); secrets excluded |
| `sync-activity-provenance` | done | `app/src/panels/changes.rs` (`AuthorFilter::Sync`, `render_activity_row`) | synced ops render as their real op + `synced from <device>` provenance; "Synced" filter pill; `AuthorClass::Sync` already in `core/src/activity.rs` |
| `sync-disable-kill-switch` | done | `app/src/main.rs::reconcile_sync` + `sync_service.rs::shutdown` + `bootstrap.rs::Spawner::spawn_sync_service` (per-service cancel token) | off by default (nothing constructed); per-frame reconcile builds/spawns the engine when `[sync].enabled` flips true and tears it down when false — both live, no vault reopen |
| `sync-quic-transport-deferred` | planned | — | QUIC transport once UDP reachability is acceptable, or as an opportunistic upgrade with TCP fallback |
| `sync-metadata-padding-deferred` | planned | — | blob padding / cover traffic to blunt the server's count/size/timing metadata view |


## Autosave (autosave.md)

Crash-recovery sidecar snapshots of dirty buffers, NPP shape (one file per buffer, overwritten in place per tick), plus a tab-state snapshot the next vault open round-trips. Backend-owned (`core::autosave`); separate from the op log.

| Slug | Status | Notes |
| ---- | ------ | ----- |
| `autosave-backend-module` | done | `core/src/autosave.rs` (`Autosave` struct, `core::autosave` module entry), the host (`autosave_*` commands as 5–15 line wrappers, `VaultSession.autosave` field). Same module-discipline shape as `core::store` — all `.hiker/autosave/` filesystem touches confined here, plain Rust types crossing the boundary |
| `autosave-store-layout` | done | `core/src/autosave.rs` (`Autosave::open` creates `<vault>/.hiker/autosave/`, `IndexFile` with `version`/`entries`/`tab_state` fields, atomic `write_file_atomic` helper write-temp-then-rename for both per-buffer `<id>--<slug>.md` files and `index.json`) | 
| `autosave-one-per-buffer` | done | `core/src/autosave.rs` (`Autosave::write` looks up existing `autosave_id` for the path and reuses it — same on-disk filename overwritten in place; `write_overwrites_in_place` test confirms id stability across ticks). NPP shape — no per-tick versioning |
| `autosave-write-tick` | done | `app/src/workbench_host.rs` (autosave tick every 5s while any tab is dirty; self-suspends when nothing's dirty and re-arms on the next dirty transition on vault open; flush-all runs synchronously on window blur). Per-path dedupe via a last-written cache so a buffer at the same content doesn't re-push every tick |
| `autosave-recover-cmd` | done | `core/src/autosave.rs` (`Autosave::recover` walks `index.json`, computes the live on-disk hash for each entry, drops matches + orphan-sidecar pointers from the index file, returns only the real deltas as `RecoveredEntry`). Host wrapper `autosave_recover` translates to `RecoveredEntryDto` for the frontend; tests `recover_drops_matching_on_disk` / `recover_surfaces_when_disk_differs` / `recover_drops_orphan_sidecar_pointer` cover the three branches |
| `autosave-recovery-auto-restore` | done | `app/src/workbench_host.rs` (recovery-and-restore opens every entry returned by `autosave_recover()` as a sticky tab — for files still on disk, loads autosaved bytes into the editor so the buffer reads dirty against `loadedText`; for deleted files, recreates the file with the autosaved bytes first, then opens normally as a clean buffer; both branches `autosave_discard` after the open since the autosaved copy is now live in memory). No modal — the dirty-marker on each restored tab is the recovery affordance, composing with the existing close-dirty modal + `editor-diff-vs-disk-toggle` |
| `autosave-tab-state-store` | done | `core/src/autosave.rs` (`TabState` with `open_paths`/`active_path`/`preview_path`/`saved_at_ms`; `save_tab_state` overwrites the slot in the same `index.json` rewrite path), `app/src/workbench_host.rs` (tab-state push debounced ~250ms; snapshots the current open-tab order + active + preview; fires on every tab open / close / activate / preview-slot change) |
| `autosave-tab-state-silent-restore` | done | `app/src/workbench_host.rs` (recovery-and-restore loads the tab state, reopens each path in `open_paths` as a sticky tab, then activates `active_path`, then opens `preview_path` if set and not already in the open set). Missing paths drop silently into a `tracing` error per spec; runs after the recovery modal resolves |
| `autosave-readonly-skipped` | done | `app/src/workbench_host.rs` (autosavable-path check filters out anything whose tab isn't a file buffer; the active-buffer text reader also short-circuits on non-file modes). Trash / snapshot previews therefore never enter the tick loop in the first place |
| `autosave-rename-clear-old` | done | `app/src/workbench_host.rs` (the watcher file events renamed branch clears the old autosave path after updating the buffer path). Next tick writes against the new path naturally — no special-case write |
| `autosave-no-watcher-suppression` | done | `core/src/watcher.rs` (`is_ignored` rejects any `.hiker/` prefix, line ~272). Autosave writes land at `.hiker/autosave/...` so they never reach the watcher's debouncer; no `Watcher::suppress_path` dance needed in `Autosave::write`. Test `is_ignored("'.hiker/index.db'")` already covers the rule |
| `autosave-vault-swap-clears` | done | `core/src/autosave.rs` (`vault_swap_reset` wipes the on-disk dir contents; `vault_swap_reset_wipes_dir` test). Each new vault gets its own `Autosave` instance constructed in `open_vault_at_inner`; `applyOpenedVault` stops the prior tick before recovery, runs `runAutosaveRecoveryAndRestore` for the new vault, then `start`s the tick. `onCloseRequested` clears each handled path + pushes empty tab state before destroy |
| `autosave-backup-class` | done | matches `design.md`'s sync/backup framing without further code changes — `.hiker/autosave/<id>--*.md` is regenerable from running memory (lost only on clean shutdown or modal Discard), `index.json` is durable (carries tab-state, not reconstructible after clean shutdown). "Back up the whole `.hiker/`" rule already covers both correctly |


## MCP (mcp.md)

All planned. v3 milestone — in-process MCP server in `mcp-server/` crate, Streamable HTTP transport, rmcp-backed, read + write tool surface, agent rollback via `core::changes`. Architectural decisions (in-process, decoupled crate, transport choice, localhost-trust auth, etc.) live in the spec; the slugs below are concrete implementable features.

| Slug | Status | Notes |
| ---- | ------ | ----- |
| `mcp-server-crate` | done | `mcp-server/` is now a library crate; `hiker_mcp::start(McpDeps) -> McpServerHandle` spawns an axum task wrapping `rmcp::StreamableHttpService`. the host (`open_vault_at_inner` → `start_mcp`) brings it up on vault open; the handle drops on session swap, cancelling the task and removing the discovery file. `mcp-server/tests/smoke.rs` exercises the full HTTP path |
| `mcp-config-section` | done | `core/src/config.rs` (`McpConfig`, `McpToolsConfig`, `McpAuditConfig`); strict-load validates the section. Defaults: enabled=true, host="127.0.0.1", port=0, discovery_file=`.hiker/mcp.json`, max_top_k=50, tools.writes_enabled=true, tools.allow_redacted_lookup=false, every per-tool `<name>_enabled=true`, audit.log_full_input=false |
| `mcp-bind-host-configurable` | done | `core/src/config.rs::McpConfig::host`; `mcp-server/src/lib.rs` builds the bind address from `host` + `port` (with IPv6 bracket handling). Default stays `127.0.0.1`. The settings UI row carries the LAN-exposure warning text in its `desc` so the consequence is visible at the choice site |
| `mcp-tool-toggles` | done | `core/src/config.rs::McpToolsConfig::tool_allowed`; `mcp-server/src/handler.rs::guard_tool` called at the top of every `*_inner`; chat-agent path also filters `agent_tool_defs_filtered(...)` so the model never sees disabled tools. Live-applied via `Arc<RwLock<McpToolsConfig>>` shared between `VaultSession.mcp_tools` and the handler — `set_setting` swaps the contents in place so the next dispatch sees the new gate without a vault restart |
| `mcp-settings-ui-section` | done | `app/src/panels/settings/mod.rs` `mcp` section — bool / string / port-int rows for every eligible key, plus a read-only display for `discovery_file`. Defaults to vault scope (matches `[tasks]`); user scope works via the per-section toggle |
| `mcp-port-discovery` | done | `mcp-server/src/discovery.rs` writes `<vault>/.hiker/mcp.json` on bind; `McpServerHandle::shutdown` (and `Drop`) remove it. OS-assigned ephemeral by default; honors `[mcp].port` for a fixed port. Smoke test asserts both write and removal |
| `mcp-dynamic-capabilities` | done | `mcp-server/src/handler.rs` (rmcp `tool_router` advertises the v3 surface) | the mechanism is in place — the rmcp router is the natural seam for conditional advertising. No `is_available()` predicate yet because no feature-gated tool has landed; future feature-gated tools own their own predicates and will plug into the same router |
| `mcp-error-model` | done | `mcp-server/src/handler.rs::translate_hiker_err` translates `HikerError` → JSON-RPC error codes. Hiker-specific: 1002 (`note_not_found`), 1003 (`drift`), 1004 (`disabled`), 1005 (`indexer_unavailable`). `1001 vault_not_open` not wired — the server only exists while a vault is open, so `vault_not_open` can't occur over the wire today; reserved for the future "MCP outlives session" mode |
| `mcp-lifecycle-aware` | partial | per-spec, the lifecycle fields (`hiker.archived` / `redacted` / `retired`) aren't yet implemented in hiker so the filter is a no-op. The redacted-body restriction lives on `core::store::get_note` rather than the MCP layer when it lands |
| `mcp-audit-log-mcp-calls` | done | `mcp-server/src/audit.rs::AuditLog` appends one JSONL row per call to `<vault>/.hiker/agent-log/<YYYY-MM-DD>.jsonl` with `surface="mcp-tool-call"`. When `[mcp.audit] log_full_input = false` (default), bulky fields (`content`, `query`, `fields`) are redacted to `{redacted: true, len: N}` |
| `mcp-tool-search-notes` | done | `mcp-server/src/handler.rs::HikerHandler::search_notes` wraps `core::search::query`; returns lexical_hits + semantic_hits + fused. `top_k` clamps the fused bucket to `[mcp] max_top_k`. Embedder unavailability surfaces as `1005 indexer_unavailable` |
| `mcp-tool-get-note` | done | `HikerHandler::get_note`; `detail = digest|snippet|full`. Snippet uses chunk 0 from the store when available, fallback head-of-file otherwise. Missing files return `1002 note_not_found` |
| `mcp-tool-related-notes` | done | `HikerHandler::related_notes` calls `Store::related_notes`; `top_k` capped by `[mcp] max_top_k`. Unindexed source returns an empty vec rather than erroring |
| `mcp-tool-write-note` | done | `core/src/ops/agent.rs::write_note` + `HikerHandler::write_note`. Watcher-suppresses, writes via `vault.write_file_checked` when `expected_hash` is set, queues a pending op-log op (`author='agent:<client_id>'`, whole-body `Replace`) via `op_writes::stage_agent_edits`, enqueues `IndexJob::Upsert` |
| `mcp-tool-edit-note` | partial | `mcp-server/src/handler/dispatch.rs::apply_edits` — span-anchored patch tool, advertised in `tool_router` + `agent_tool_defs_filtered`. Direct mode (review off) validates + applies all edits transactionally via `apply_edit` and routes through `agent_write_note` once. Review-on mode now stages op-log pending ops via `core::ops::op_writes::stage_agent_edits` (one anchored `Replace` per edit, all sharing the returned `batch_id`, author `agent:<client-id>`), reviewed through the op-log surfaces re-homed in Phases 3b–3d — replaces the prior `Staging::propose_batch`. A `replace_all` edit with >1 match collapses the call to one anchorless whole-body op (the op-log anchor must resolve uniquely). **Partial**: rule 5 (read-before-write) is owned by `mcp-read-before-write` (still planned); the per-tool `edit_note_enabled` toggle row is wired in the settings UI |
| `mcp-edit-note-validation` | partial | `mcp-server/src/handler.rs::edit_note_inner` enforces rules 1–4: path exists (else `1002`), per-edit anchor unique unless `replace_all: true` (else `1003` for missing, `invalid_params` for non-unique), no overlap between resolved byte ranges across edits (`invalid_params` naming the offending pair), all anchors resolve against the pre-application file content (single read up front; never sequential dependency). Smoke tests cover each branch. **Partial**: rule 5 (per-session read set) lands with `mcp-read-before-write` |
| `mcp-author-stamp-on-create-only` | planned | drop the automatic `hiker.author: agent-authored` stamp on every agent write; stamp only when `write_note` creates a previously-non-existent path. `edit_note` / `set_frontmatter` / `apply_tag` never stamp. Updates `mcp-tool-write-note` / `mcp-tool-set-frontmatter` / `mcp-tool-apply-tag-remove-tag` to drop the stamp call |
| `mcp-read-before-write` | planned | per-session `ReadSet` in `mcp-server/src/handler.rs` populated by successful `get_note` calls; `edit_note` always requires a prior read, `write_note` requires one when target path exists on disk (creates exempt). Violations return `1008 read_required` before validation. Foot-gun guard against hallucinated-anchor edits |
| `mcp-tool-set-frontmatter` | done | new `core/src/frontmatter.rs` (split/merge/assemble); `core::ops::agent_set_frontmatter` reads existing, deep-merges patch via `merge_agent_patch`, stamps `hiker.author: agent-authored`, routes through `agent_write_note`. Errors `invalid_params` if `fields` isn't a JSON object |
| `mcp-ui-refresh-on-agent-write` | done | `app/src/workbench_host.rs` (agent-write handler) — listens on op-log append events, gates on `author.startsWith("agent:")`, and runs the same tree-refresh / vault-home / active-buffer-reload sequence the watcher handler does. Dirty buffer is kept (toast surfaces the conflict) rather than prompting modally — agent writes are server-driven so an interrupt prompt would surprise the user |
| `mcp-tool-apply-tag-remove-tag` | done | `core::ops::agent_apply_tag` / `agent_remove_tag` thin wrappers over `agent_set_frontmatter` operating on the `tags` list. Idempotent (no-op when tag is already present / absent) |
| `mcp-write-tools-staging-aware` | partial | `mcp-server/src/handler/dispatch.rs::save_note` / `merge_frontmatter` / `update_tag` stage an op-log pending op (via `op_writes::stage_agent_edits`, no more `core::staging`) when `[mcp.tools].review_required` is on and return `status: "staged"` + `staging_id`. Tool description strings call out the staged behavior so agents don't read a staged write as a failed write. **Partial**: (a) the agent-facing introspection tools (`mcp-tool-list-pending-proposals`, `mcp-tool-get-pending-proposal`) are specced but not implemented; (b) `mcp-tool-edit-note` adds `propose_batch` and per-edit proposals per `staging-per-edit-proposals` |
| `mcp-staging-read-disk-only` | done | `HikerHandler::get_note` resolves `vault.abs_path(rel_path)` and returns `1002 note_not_found` when the file doesn't exist on disk; pending proposals are not transparently surfaced. Agents discover staged content via the introspection tools, keeping the disk-vs-staging boundary explicit |
| `mcp-tool-list-pending-proposals` | planned | wraps `core::staging::list()` with default `surface = "mcp-tool-call"` so the agent sees only MCP-originated proposals. Returns id + target_path + action + surface + session_id + created_at + content_hash; no body. Read-only; honors per-tool toggle. Lets an agent confirm its staged write landed when `get_note` returns 1002 |
| `mcp-tool-get-pending-proposal` | planned | wraps `core::staging::get(id)`; returns full metadata + proposed body. `1002`-style error when id is unknown (accepted / rejected / never existed). Agents cannot accept or reject from MCP — that stays a human-in-the-loop action |
| `mcp-pending-proposal-anchor-status` | planned | extends `get_pending_proposal` response: for `edit_note`-shaped proposals, recompute anchor match state on the fly against buffer-if-open-else-disk and return an `anchors: [{ edit_index, anchor_status: "holds"\|"drifted"\|"ambiguous", old_str_preview }]` array plus `target: "buffer"\|"disk"`. Per-edit, joint-batch (resolved via shared `batch_id`); omitted entirely for whole-file shapes. Racy by construction (true at read time only). Lets the agent detect doomed staged edits in a concurrent-edit workflow and amend before the user clicks Accept, instead of finding out via drift-at-apply |
| `mcp-tool-amend-pending-proposal` | planned | new MCP write tool letting an agent revise its own pending proposal in place before the user reviews. Same-client only; whole-file shapes only (`write_note` / `set_frontmatter` / `apply_tag`); `edit_note` batch-shape amend deferred. No version history — overwrites the stored body, bumps `metadata.amended_at_ms` + `metadata.amend_count`, recomputes `content_hash`. Fires staging-snapshot updates so an open review surface re-renders. User-accept races resolve in the staging table's transaction (1002 to the amend). Per-tool toggle `[mcp.tools].amend_pending_proposal_enabled`. Unblocks the "agent realized its first attempt was wrong" workflow without breaking the one-human-gate-per-change model |
| `mcp-propose-plugin` | planned | scaffold a plugin into `.hiker/plugins-scratch/<id>/` (manifest + source files); idempotent per id; refuses scratch-dir escapes; nothing loaded/trusted yet. Write-class, honors per-tool toggle. Lands with the plugin-authoring loop (`plugins.md`) |
| `mcp-install-plugin` | planned | hand a scaffolded plugin (Lua source or self-built `.wasm`) into `plugin-install-flow`: reads manifest `runtime`, computes blake3 pins, enters disabled pending the human consent dialog (`plugin-authoring-proposal-gate`), never auto-enabled. Returns `{status:"pending_review", manifest_hash, source_hash}` |
| `mcp-fetch-raw` | planned | source-plugin authoring tool: fetch a sample page's raw HTML (+ data-blobs) over the static path (`extract-web-static-fetch`); target is a user-supplied/confirmed URL, capping agent fetch reach |
| `mcp-extract-preview` | planned | source-plugin authoring tool: run a candidate extractor (built-in or draft plugin) against a URL/file → `{markdown,next_urls}` without vault writes; candidate runs sandboxed so it's safe to invoke freely |
| `mcp-authoring-scoped-subtask` | planned | the authoring tools run in a host-enforced reduced-capability sub-task (fetch+preview+propose, no vault-write/arbitrary-net) so untrusted page content can't become a consequential action; task wiring, not a togglable mode. Detail in `plugins.md` `plugin-authoring-security` |
| `mcp-tool-trails-list` | planned | enumerate trails with optional filters (containing-note, recently-activated, name-substring); returns id + title + waypoint count + activation timestamp + path. Lands with `trails.md` |
| `mcp-tool-trail-get` | planned | fetch a trail's full body + ordered waypoint list (each waypoint's source ref + annotation body); detail levels `digest` / `full`. Lands with `trails.md` |
| `mcp-tool-trails-containing-note` | planned | reverse lookup; returns trails that include a given note as a waypoint. Lands with `trails.md` |
| `mcp-tool-trail-create` | planned | create a new trail (or draft trail when `draft=true`); placement per `trails-default-location` (or `.hiker/trails/drafts/` for drafts); routes through `core::ops::agent_*` so writes appear in `core::changes` with `author='agent:<client-id>'`; default-draft when `agent-write-review-mode` is on per `trail-draft-from-agent` |
| `mcp-tool-trail-append-waypoint` | planned | append a waypoint to a trail; `parent_waypoint_id` arg makes the new waypoint a side-trail child of the given parent (omitted → root-level append); creates the waypoint-note under `.hiker/trails/<trail-id>/waypoints/`, links to source, seeds optional starter annotation (default empty body); same agent-write routing as `mcp-tool-trail-create` |
| `mcp-tool-trail-remove-waypoint` | planned | remove a waypoint from a trail; cascades to descendants when target has children (per `trails-mode-remove-waypoint-verb`'s cascade rule); routes waypoint-note delete through `core::ops::delete` |
| `mcp-tool-boards-list` | done | `mcp-server/src/handler/{router,dispatch}.rs` (`boards_list` → `enumerate_boards`) wraps `core::boards::list`; read-only; per-tool toggle `mcp.tools.boards_list_enabled` |
| `mcp-tool-board-get` | done | `mcp-server/src/handler/{router,dispatch}.rs` (`board_get` → `fetch_board`) wraps `core::boards::get_board` (body + resolved columns/cards); per-tool toggle `mcp.tools.board_get_enabled` |
| `mcp-tool-board-add-card` | done | `mcp-server/src/handler/{router,dispatch}.rs` (`board_add_card` → `add_board_card`); idempotent per board (`status:"noop"`); review mode stages the board-doc edit via `stage_whole_body` (`status:"staged"`), direct mode commits via `op_writes::user_save` (`status:"written"`) using the read-only `core::boards::add_card_preview` to avoid holding the `!Send` Store across an await; per-tool toggle `mcp.tools.board_add_card_enabled`. KNOWN GAP: unlike the UI/core `add_card`, this path does NOT lazy-stamp an unstamped source note's ULID (the Send-safe preview is read-only), so the card anchors path-only until the note is stamped elsewhere — folded into the still-`partial` `note-id-stamping` work |

Pathology mix (near-dup / topic-drift / very-short / very-long) at ~10%. Runner / scoring / recall@K still deferred until `cli-query` lands |
