## Extraction (extract.md)

First concrete slice of design.md's extractor pipeline: the decoupled `hiker-extract` crate + sidecar write path + the PDF and website-to-markdown extractors + the governed crawl loop. Sources are viewed in the user's OS apps, not rendered in hiker. All planned; lands v4+ per design.md build order (non-md files are silently ignored until then per `index.md` vault tolerance). The byte→text long tail is the source-plugin surface in `## Plugins`.

| Slug | Status | Notes |
| ---- | ------ | ----- |
| `extract-preserve-original` | planned | original source byte-for-byte preserved — never modified/moved/deleted; hiker owns only the sidecar; source deletion orphans the sidecar rather than removing it |
| `extract-crate-decoupled` | planned | extraction is a leaf crate `hiker-extract` (PDF/HTTP/HTML deps live only there); `core` does not depend on it — the sidecar `.md` on disk is the seam, ingested by the existing watcher |
| `extract-registry` | planned | built-in trait registry routes a source (ext/MIME/URL) to the first matching extractor; one module + one registration line per type under `hiker_extract::*`; no plugin loading on this path |
| `extract-contract-next-urls` | planned | `Extracted = {markdown, frontmatter?, archive?, next_urls[]}`; emitting `next_urls` makes any extractor crawl-capable, consumed by the frontier loop; empty = plain one-page extractor |
| `extract-fallback-chain` | planned | `extract()` returns `Result<Option<Extracted>>`; matched-but-can't-handle returns `Ok(None)` and the registry tries the next match (PDF fast-path → fallback) |
| `extract-per-source-override` | planned | `hiker.extractor: <name>` in sidecar frontmatter pins a specific extractor, bypassing match order |
| `extract-version-cache-key` | planned | extracted-content cache keyed on (source hash, extractor name, `version()`); bumping a version re-extracts that type — mirrors `embedder-version-tag` |
| `extract-trigger-auto-glob` | planned | `[extract].auto_globs` (default empty) folders auto-extract non-md sources on appear/change via the startup scan + watcher (extends `ingest-startup-scan`/`ingest-watcher-driven`); no whole-vault auto-extraction |
| `extract-trigger-on-demand` | planned | non-md outside the auto-globs extracts only via an explicit filetree "Make searchable" action (+ extract-on-open); one-off extract job per file |
| `extract-trigger-default-ignore` | planned | non-md neither auto-glob-matched nor explicitly extracted stays ignored — keeps the ⊘ unsupported marker, opens via the OS handler (today's behavior) |
| `extract-sidecar-write` | planned | sidecar at `<full-source-filename>.md` alongside the source; `hiker:` frontmatter (source/source_sha256/source_mtime/type/storage: sidecar); indexer ingests it as ordinary `.md` |
| `extract-sidecar-provenance` | planned | sidecars stamp `hiker.author: imported` + a specific `hiker.provenance` label (`pdf`, `web-scrape`, …) per design.md provenance axis |
| `extract-sidecar-linked-state` | planned | `hiker.link_state: linked` (RO, re-extraction overwrites) default; "Unlink from source" → RW, re-extraction stops; re-link re-extracts behind a confirm. Mirrors design.md |
| `extract-sidecar-tree-hidden` | planned | `*.<ext>.md` sidecars hidden in the file tree by default; surfaced via "view extracted text" on the original + in search results |
| `extract-open-original-external` | planned | hiker renders extracted markdown only; "view original" opens the source in the OS handler (browser for the archived page, system viewer for PDF) — no in-app web/PDF renderer, no embedded engine |
| `extract-pdf-fast-path` | planned | v1 PDF: pure-Rust text extraction crate → sidecar; no external `pdftotext` binary, no bundled poppler — preserves single-binary / clean-SBOM posture |
| `extract-pdf-scanned-detect` | planned | empty/garbage fast-path output (scanned/image-only PDF) returns `Ok(None)` for the fallback chain; records a skip reason when no fallback configured |
| `extract-pdf-command-escape` | planned | users wire `pdftotext`/`marker`/`docling` via design.md's per-glob `CommandExtractor` for higher fidelity before a native fallback lands |
| `extract-pdf-marker-fallback` | planned | deferred — built-in marker/docling fallback for scanned/complex-layout PDFs behind the fast path |
| `extract-image-ocr` | planned | deferred — OCR (tesseract) image extractor; same registry, separate module |
| `extract-audio-transcribe` | planned | deferred — whisper.cpp audio extractor; same registry, separate module |
| `extract-web-static-fetch` | planned | website→md via one HTTP GET (`reqwest`+`rustls`); no JavaScript, no headless browser |
| `extract-web-readability` | planned | `html5ever` DOM + readability main-content isolation → markdown sidecar body |
| `extract-web-data-blob` | planned | parse (never execute) `__NEXT_DATA__` / `__NUXT__` / JSON-LD blobs in the static HTML to reach framework-rendered "SPA" sites without their JS |
| `extract-web-archive-singlefile` | planned | captured page saved as a self-contained single-file `.html` (subresources inlined as data URIs, scripts stripped) via a native-Rust archiver; the offline-openable canonical artifact `extract-open-original-external` opens |
| `extract-web-fallbacks` | planned | thin-page fallback chain: declared full-text RSS/Atom → AMP variant (`rel=amphtml`) → print view; first with real content wins |
| `extract-web-versioned` | planned | `hiker scrape`/`refresh` re-fetch; a changed re-fetch lands as an `extractor` op on the sidecar (`extract-version-oplog`), identical content is a no-op — history/diff from op-log surfaces, no bespoke version store |
| `extract-version-oplog` | planned | re-extraction versioning IS the op-log: an `extractor` op on the sidecar's `accepted` (`op-log-reextract-replace`); history/diff/restore/version-dropdown reuse `core::changes`; "versions" = op-log history filtered to extractor+user ops |
| `extract-artifact-retention` | planned | binary-artifact retention cascade (op-log versions text, not blobs): vault `[extract] artifact_retention` → per-crawl/glob → per-source frontmatter; `latest` (default) / `keep:N` / `forever`; retained under `.hiker/refs/<doc_id>/` keyed by op, device-local |
| `extract-web-no-js-stance` | planned | explicit boundary: no JS execution in hiker. SPA-only / authenticated-XHR / server-paywall content served by source plugins (API-fetch) or the external manifest-import path, never an embedded engine |
| `scrape-cmd` | planned | `hiker scrape <url>` / `hiker refresh` thin CLI adapters over the web extractor; stamp `web-scrape` provenance + `source_url`/`captured_at` |
| `crawl-frontier-loop` | planned | the crawler: a governed queue of URLs draining the extractor contract — pop → extract → admit its `next_urls` → repeat to limits. Not a separate engine |
| `crawl-governance` | planned | scope/dedup/depth/page-count/rate/robots all live in the loop, written once — the extractor proposes links, the loop decides which to walk; no extractor can runaway-crawl |
| `crawl-modes` | planned | list (multi-seed, depth 0) / hub (single seed, depth 1) / deep (depth N + patterns) are loop parameters, not separate modes |
| `crawl-scope-patterns` | planned | gitignore-style follow-pattern ("continue into links matching X") + extract-pattern ("keep pages matching Y"); regex escape hatch if globs too blunt on URLs |
| `crawl-extract-seed-flag` | planned | whether the seed/hub page itself becomes a sidecar; default off for list/hub (launcher page), on for deep |
| `crawl-link-rewrite-wikilinks` | planned | loop holds the `URL → sidecar` map; final pass rewrites internal links to `wikilinks.md`'s `[[<ulid>\|title]]` form + stamps page ULIDs (`wikilink-id-form`/`wikilink-target-stamp`); non-crawled + external stay URLs; emits syntax now, clickable once wikilink rendering lands |
| `crawl-list-from-note` | planned | list-mode seed set pulled from a note's links (or selection) — "extract all links in this note" from the tree/editor UI |
| `crawl-job-note` | planned | a crawl is a saved, re-runnable manifest note; frontmatter holds all params (seed/mode/depth/patterns/extractor/dest/retention/rate), body holds run log + captured-page index |
| `crawl-job-form` | planned | "New crawl…" hamburger item opens a crawl tab rendering a form over the job note's frontmatter (Trails-mode pattern, not a modal) + seed input + extractor picker + Run/cancel + live progress; run launches, re-run re-crawls |
| `crawl-child-parent` | planned | each captured page stamps `hiker.parent: <job-ulid>` (design.md manifest-parent precedent) — a logical parent-child independent of on-disk location; presentation is `vault-view.md`'s job |
| `crawl-task-queue-lane` | planned | a crawl runs as a `task-queue.md` job on a non-LLM worker lane; per-page extractions are child tasks grouped under the parent crawl (one rolled-up queue row); cancel/progress reuse the queue |
| `extract-manifest-import` | planned | escape hatch for JS-heavy crawls: external tool (Playwright/Scrapy) runs the crawl, hands hiker a dir + `manifest.json` (`{url,output_file,links}`); hiker places sidecars + rewrites wikilinks + versions. JS runs only in the user's tool |
| `extract-source-plugin-boundary` | planned | the unbounded byte→text tail (per-site scrapers, niche text formats) is `plugins.md`'s source-plugin surface, deliberately distinct from this binary-capable built-in registry |


## Vault view (vault-view.md)

A second sidebar mode presenting the vault as a read-only logical lens over the on-disk Files tree — the follow-on that makes crawl-job nesting and source grouping legible. All planned; lands after the crawl pipeline.

| Slug | Status | Notes |
| ---- | ------ | ----- |
| `vault-view-mode` | planned | fourth sidebar mode via `sidebar-mode-switcher` (Files / Cluster trees / Trails / Vault); Files unchanged + default; persists under `vault.sidebar_mode`; shares the pinned Trash + `+`/`⋯` row |
| `vault-view-readonly-lens` | planned | Vault mode is a read-only lens derived from frontmatter + index; never moves/renames files (no drag-to-reorganize); the filesystem stays authoritative for placement |
| `vault-view-crawl-nesting` | planned | crawl-job notes render with captured pages nested via `hiker.parent` (`crawl-child-parent`), independent of on-disk location; stable across re-crawls |
| `vault-view-sidecar-surfacing` | planned | source + its extracted sidecar render as one nested entry (vs hidden in Files mode per `extract-sidecar-tree-hidden`) |
| `vault-view-source-groups` | planned | virtual top-level groups by source type + authorship trichotomy (`hiker.author`/`hiker.provenance`); the Vault-mode realization of `tree-source-visibility-toggles`; lens selectable from `⋯` |
| `vault-view-membership-groups` | planned | deferred — trail / cluster membership as additional Vault-mode groupings |
| `vault-view-saved-lenses` | planned | deferred — user-defined grouping rules (tag facet / folder glob / frontmatter field) saved and re-selectable |


## Wikilinks (wikilinks.md)

ID-based note references authored/read as names, stored as `[[<ulid>|<display>]]`. Page-level links land: parsing + id-detection in `core/src/wikilink.rs`, name-normalize-on-save (with lazy target stamping) in `core::ops::buffer::normalize_wikilinks`, live-title clickable pills in `editor/editor-md/src/links.rs` + the app resolver, click-to-open / create-on-click in `app/src/panels/buffer/mod.rs`. Headings/blocks + embeds stay deferred.

| Slug | Status | Notes |
| ---- | ------ | ----- |
| `wikilink-id-form` | done | `core/src/wikilink.rs` (`parse_links`, `looks_like_ulid`) | stored form `[[<ulid>\|<display>]]` produced by name-normalize-on-save; ULID is the durable target (survives renames/moves incl. hiker's own auto-moves), display is the plain-viewer fallback; no referrer-rewrite engine needed |
| `wikilink-render` | done | `editor/editor-md/src/links.rs` (`wikilink_decorations()`) | cursor-aware decoration: off-line → clickable styled link pill, on-line → raw markdown revealed. Now wired to the live-title resolver |
| `wikilink-render-live-title` | done | `editor/editor-md/src/links.rs` (`WikilinkWidget`, `TitleResolver`) + `app/src/panels/buffer/mod.rs` (`resolve_title` closure) | rendered label is the target's current title resolved by ULID via `Store::path_for_id`, not the stored `\|display`; renaming the target updates every link's label on the next decoration rebuild; falls back display → raw target when unresolved. Note: the wikilink decoration cache keys on doc/cursor-line/viewport, so a target rename refreshes the label on the next edit/scroll rather than instantly — acceptable, flagged as a follow-up if instant refresh is wanted |
| `wikilink-autocomplete` | done | `app/src/completion_sources.rs` (`WikilinkSource`) | `[[` opens the title picker (shared `CompletionSource` infra); selection inserts `[[<title>]]`. The id-form materializes on the next save via `wikilink-name-normalize` (the sync `CompletionSource` commit can't run async stamping at pick-time), so the user never has to type a ULID. Trade-off: a picked note whose basename collides with another stays a name link until disambiguated — rare; surfaced as unresolved |
| `wikilink-name-normalize` | done | `core/src/ops/buffer.rs` (`normalize_wikilinks`) + `app/src/editor_pane.rs` (`save_buffer`, `normalize_wikilinks_blocking`) | a name target (hand-typed or external-editor) resolves by unique title/path match and rewrites to `[[<ulid>\|Name]]` on save, stamping the target's `hiker.id`; ambiguous/no-match left untouched (stays unresolved, no guessing); self-links skipped. Cheap parse pre-check keeps the common save off the store lock + async bridge |
| `wikilink-resolve-store` | done | `core/src/store/notes.rs` (`path_for_id`/`id_for_path`) | click/resolve maps ULID → current path via the indexer `path → id` table, independent of file location |
| `wikilink-target-stamp` | done | reuses `core::ops::buffer::ensure_note_id_stamped` from `normalize_wikilinks` | creating a link stamps the target's `hiker.id` — the lazy trigger of `note-id-stamping`; adopts the indexer's existing `path_ids` ULID so the two id systems stay in lockstep |
| `wikilink-click-open` | done | `app/src/panels/buffer/mod.rs` (`open_wikilink_at`) | a tagged `WidgetClick` carries the link span byte; re-parses + resolves + opens via the shared open-note path (preview slot default, Mod-click sticky, switch-if-open per `editor-preview-tab-from-open-callsites` / `multi-buffer-tree-click-switches-tab`) |
| `wikilink-backlinks` | done | `app/src/panels/backlinks.rs` (`scan_backlinks`) | matches both an id-form link whose ULID equals the active note's stamped id (resolved via `Store::id_for_path`, survives target moves) and a lenient name-form match. Implemented as the existing cached content scan rather than a dedicated structural-index edge table — the typed-edge index is still future work |
| `wikilink-unresolved` | done | `editor/editor-md/src/links.rs` (`COLOR_WIKILINK_UNRESOLVED`) + `app/src/panels/buffer/mod.rs` (`create_and_open_wikilink_target`) | a dangling ULID renders in the distinct unresolved color; clicking an unresolved name creates the note + opens it (the link normalizes to its id on the next save); clicking a dangling ULID surfaces a "target missing" toast |
| `wikilink-headings-blocks` | planned | deferred — `[[<ulid>#Heading]]` / `[[<ulid>#^block]]` (block needs an auto-injected anchor in the target) |
| `wikilink-embed` | planned | deferred — `![[<ulid>]]` transclusion rendering the target's content/image/PDF inline |


## Plugins (plugins.md)

| Slug | Status | Evidence | Notes |
| ---- | ------ | -------- | ----- |
| `plugin-host` | done | `core/src/plugins/mod.rs` (`PluginHost`), `core/src/plugins/error.rs` | Orchestrates load → verify → instantiate → init; holds loaded instances + latest VDOM per plugin; `load_enabled` reads `plugins.json`. No `pub use` re-export farm |
| `plugin-runtime-wasmi` | done | `core/src/plugins/runtime.rs` (`WasmEngine` trait + `WasmiEngine`) | Swappable engine trait; `wasmi` pure-Rust interpreter is the only impl, confined here. Chosen over wasmtime for clean SBOM (no JIT); wasmtime/Component-Model is the future swap |
| `plugin-host-call` | done | `core/src/plugins/runtime.rs` (`host_call_shim` + fat-pipe marshalling), `core/src/plugins/dispatch.rs` (`HostInvoker`, `HostApi`, `StoreHostApi`) | `host_call(name,args_json)->result_json` over linear memory (plugin exports `memory`+`plugin_alloc`, packed `ptr<<32\|len`). `notes.query` wraps `store-note-query`; `notes.list` + `log.*` also wired |
| `plugin-permissions` | done | `core/src/plugins/manifest.rs` (`Permissions`), `core/src/plugins/dispatch.rs` (`required_permission`, `HostInvoker::invoke`) | Capability-scoped, fail-closed: each host call maps to a required permission; missing grant or unknown call is refused (delivered to the plugin as a JSON error, not silent). `log.*` is free |
| `plugin-manifest` | done | `core/src/plugins/manifest.rs` (`Manifest`, `UiDecl`, `SidebarPanelDecl`) | `manifest.json` schema: id/name/version/permissions/ui/entry; immutable per identity |
| `plugin-hash-pin` | done | `core/src/plugins/manifest.rs` (`PluginsFile`, `PinnedEntry`, `blake3_pin`, `verify_pin`) | `plugins.json` pins manifest + wasm by blake3; a mismatch aborts load (no running changed code against an old pin) |
| `plugin-vdom` | done | `core/src/plugins/vdom.rs` (`Node`, `Row`, `TextStyle`) | Declarative VDOM the plugin returns; bounded primitive set (vstack/hstack/text/text_input/button/note_link/list/divider/spacer). Host renders natively |
| `plugin-resource-limits` | done | `core/src/plugins/runtime.rs` (`FUEL_PER_CALL`, `consume_fuel` + per-call `set_fuel`) | Fuel budget per entry call so a runaway plugin traps. Memory cap + host-call timeout still to layer on |
| `plugin-vdom-egui-renderer` | done | `app/src/panels/plugin_panel.rs` (`show_loaded` + `render_node`), `app/src/panels/plugins.rs` (renders loaded panels above the manifest editor), `app/src/state.rs` + `app/src/bootstrap.rs` (`VaultSession.plugins`, `build_plugin_host`) | egui renderer for the VDOM `Node` tree; element events route via `PluginHost::dispatch_event`, `note_link` clicks resolve through `path_for_id` → `editor_pane::open_file` |
| `plugin-notes-query-example` | done | `plugins/notes-query/` (excluded from the workspace; host-target logic tests pass) | dataview-style plugin: `build_query_args` / `build_vdom` pure logic + `#[cfg(wasm32)]` ABI shim; manifest + README with the `wasm32-unknown-unknown` build + `plugins.json` hash-pin install. Needs the wasm toolchain to produce the artifact |
| `plugin-install-flow` | done | `app/src/panels/plugins.rs` (master/detail manager) | Install-from-folder picks a plugin dir (`rfd`), parses + blake3-pins manifest + wasm, presents the requested permissions for consent, and on accept copies it under `.hiker/plugins/<id>/` + writes the `plugins.json` entry (disabled). Left list of installed plugins (+ pending review) with status dots; right detail shows manifest, a plain-language permissions list (network/write tinted), status, and Enable / Disable / Reload / Remove (live load/unload via `PluginHost`). A loaded plugin's panel renders inline in the detail |
| `plugin-manager-detail-panel` | done | `app/src/panels/plugins.rs` (`detail_installed`), `app/src/panels/plugin_panel.rs` (`render_plugin`) | The selected loaded plugin's live VDOM panel renders in the detail pane under its info/permissions/actions |
| `plugin-runtime-field` | planned | — | Manifest `runtime` field selects the tier: `wasm` (prebuilt) or `lua` (script); both share manifest/permissions/hash-pin/`host_call`/VDOM/`tools` |
| `plugin-runtime-lua` | planned | — | Bundled reference C-Lua compiled freestanding to wasm32 (no WASI, `os`/`io`/`package` stripped), run under `wasmi`; vendored build artifact, no runtime dep. `piccolo`/`mlua` rejected |
| `plugin-script-source` | planned | — | `lua`-tier plugins ship source + manifest; `load_script(ptr,len)` export feeds source; hash-pin covers source; interpreter versioned with hiker; manifest records `lua_version` |
| `plugin-script-require` | planned | — | `require`/multi-file Lua resolves via a custom searcher over the plugin bundle's files, never the filesystem |
| `plugin-no-host-compile` | planned | — | Hiker never compiles, bundles no compiler, assumes no toolchain; compilation lives in the author's env (`wasm` tier) or is avoided (`lua` tier) |
| `plugin-mcp-tools` | planned | — | Manifest `tools` (`{name,description,input_schema}`) registers MCP tools dispatched into the plugin instance; advertised via `mcp-dynamic-capabilities` while enabled |
| `plugin-mcp-tools-permission` | planned | — | Providing MCP tools requires `provide:mcp-tool` permission, distinct from the consume-side `mcp:invoke` |
| `plugin-host-mcp-seam` | planned | — | `mcp-server` holds a `PluginHost` handle and calls one `dispatch_tool(plugin_id, tool, args)` edge; mirrors the `IndexerHandle` boundary, no internals crossing |
| `plugin-authoring-proposal-gate` | planned | — | Agent-authored plugins enter as proposals through `plugin-install-flow`, never auto-enabled; human consent + sandbox + approved caps bound the blast radius |
| `plugin-source-extractor` | planned | — | source plugin = URL/bytes→markdown fetcher/transform (not a binary extractor); registers into `extract.md`'s registry, returns `{markdown, frontmatter?}` into the same sidecar/versioned write path, stamps `imported` |
| `plugin-source-matcher` | planned | — | manifest declares a URL-pattern / extension matcher; advertised to the extractor registry after built-ins (built-ins win on overlap unless `hiker.extractor` names the plugin) |
| `plugin-source-api-fetch` | planned | — | per-site scraper handles a client-rendered site by calling its backing JSON API directly via `net:<host>` + `http.fetch` — does what the page's JS would, without executing it |
| `plugin-source-permission` | planned | — | `provide:source-extractor` capability, distinct from UI/tool caps; a scraper additionally requests the specific `net:<host>` it fetches |
| `plugin-source-cache-version` | planned | — | manifest `version` feeds `extract-version-cache-key`; extraction cached per source-version, so `wasmi`'s no-JIT slowness is paid once — for the per-site/per-format tail, not bulk reprocessing |
| `plugin-source-defensive-parse` | planned | — | parsing hostile input in the fuel-bounded sandbox (`plugin-resource-limits`) is safer than native in-process parsing; a security gain on top of portability |
| `plugin-authoring-fetch-raw` | planned | — | authoring-loop tool: pull a sample page's raw HTML (+ data-blobs) so the agent sees the structure; user-supplied/confirmed URL (caps fetch reach). Specced in `mcp.md` as `mcp-fetch-raw` |
| `plugin-authoring-extract-preview` | planned | — | authoring-loop tool: run a candidate extractor against a URL/file → `{markdown,next_urls}` without vault writes; runs in the `wasmi` sandbox so it's safe to invoke freely. `mcp.md` `mcp-extract-preview` |
| `plugin-authoring-security` | planned | — | host-enforced scoped sub-task (fetch+preview+propose, no vault-write/arbitrary-net) + sandboxed preview + user-confirmed fetch + human gate at install; defends by consequence, not content review. Generalization parked as `agent-session-partitions` |

## Ideas (ideas.md)

Deferred ideas with named slugs but no spec doc of their own yet. Each row points at the `ideas.md` entry for context; a real spec doc will land if/when the feature is picked up.

| Slug | Status | Notes |
| ---- | ------ | ----- |
| `browser-extension-capture` | planned | companion browser extension with two primary buttons — "Save to Hiker" (one-click capture into `inbox/` via the existing scrape/extractor pipeline) and "Save to Hiker and append to active trail" (same capture, plus append as waypoint to the active trail, greyed when no active trail). Talks to the running app over the existing localhost MCP server with a new `scrape` tool; no new transport. Phone share-sheet / Android intent capture is the sibling future on mobile. Anchor: `ideas.md` "Capture surfaces" |
| `drawio-source-ingest` | planned | future ingestion source — `.drawio` (and `.drawio.svg` / `.drawio.png`) diagrams as a source type; structural skeleton (nodes, edges, labels) extracted into a derived hiker note for search/related-notes coverage; diagram file kept as canonical artifact. Anchor: `ideas.md` "Source ingestion" |
| `drawio-mcp-tools` | planned | future MCP tool family for drawio diagrams — `get_diagram` / `add_node` / `add_edge` / `update_node`; lets an attached agent read and incrementally edit the diagram file in place via the drawio XML format. Layout round-tripping is the hard part; v1 likely restricted to structural-only edits. Anchor: `ideas.md` "Source ingestion" |
| `cluster-chunk-thread-hint` | planned | parallel chunk-level clustering: tightly-clustered chunks across notes surface as "thread" hints (not auto-trails). Anchor: `ideas.md` "Clustering" |
| `cluster-chunk-multitopic-flag` | planned | parallel chunk-level clustering: a note whose chunks scatter across clusters is a split candidate. Anchor: `ideas.md` "Clustering" |
| `servo-warc-capture` | planned | high-fidelity WARC capture+replay above the single-file HTML archive (`extract-web-archive-singlefile`); full dynamic capture needs the external manifest-import path; md sidecar stays search, WARC the fidelity layer under versioned sources, replayed in an external viewer. Anchor: `ideas.md` "Viewers" |
| `agent-session-partitions` | planned | enforced partitions so no agent session holds all three prompt-injection trifecta legs at once (untrusted content + vault-write + arbitrary-net); generalization of `plugin-authoring-security`. Touches `mcp.md`/chat/`task-queue.md`/`plugins.md`; own spec when picked up. Anchor: `ideas.md` "Agent session capabilities" |
